│ ADR-047 conflict: publication races persistence.                              
│ The assignment publishes before durable append.                               
│ ADR-047 requires persist, then publish.                                       
│ A crash between those steps loses the item.                                   
│ Lena: amend the story acceptance order.                                       
│ Jun: keep the owner lock through publication.                                 
│ Do not acknowledge on arrival or passive View.                                
│ Retain the peer-tainted context boundary.                                     
│ Review is local; only an operator may Send.

--- actual painted second-spoof-review-full-34 (80x24) ---
Proposed message (not sent) · architecture_update                               
To: lena-po, jun-dev                                                            
│ ADR-047 conflict: publication races persistence.                              
│ The assignment publishes before durable append.                               
│ ADR-047 requires persist, then publish.                                       
│ A crash between those steps loses the item.                                   
│ Lena: amend the story acceptance order.                                       
│ Jun: keep the owner lock through publication.                                 
│ Do not acknowledge on arrival or passive View.                                
│ Retain the peer-tainted context boundary.                                     
│ Review is local; only an operator may Send.                                   
│ Preserve ordered recipients and exact UTF-8 bytes.
